\texttt{Trial \#1: New plan: I failed because I got stuck in an infinite loop of swapping items between the pantry and kitchen without ever actually brewing the potion. I collected the necessary ingredients (water, red beetle, spiderweb, sandwich) and tools (ice wand, fairy wand) but never executed the recipe steps in the kitchen. Specifically, I never poured the water, added the beetle, or used the wands on the cauldron. Next time, once I have the required items in my inventory (or can access them), I will immediately go to the kitchen and execute the recipe steps in order: 1) Pour water into the cauldron, 2) Add the red beetle, 3) Wiggle the fairy wand, 4) Wiggle the ice wand, 5) Add the spiderweb, and 6) Add the sandwich. I will manage my inventory by dropping items only when necessary to pick up the next ingredient, but I will prioritize performing the "add" and "wiggle" actions on the cauldron as soon as the corresponding item is in hand, rather than hoarding items or shuttling back and forth unnecessarily.} \\
\\
